TOOL 13 | render_slide
------------------------------------------------------------------------
description:
Render one authorized slide in isolated Chromium. Use read_image(slide_id) only to revisit a valid existing screenshot; content changes require a new render.

input_schema:
  additionalProperties: false
  properties:
    slide_id:
      description: Stable ID of a page with saved HTML to render and inspect, within the current run's authorized page scope. Use the outline's slide_id, not a page number.
      pattern: ^sli_[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$
      type: string
  required:
    - slide_id
  type: object

Output contract (JSON Schema for returned text; x-content-kind identifies the successful response content, x-error-schema describes error replies; these annotations are not returned fields):
  additionalProperties: false
  description: Actual screenshot image block plus this JSON diagnostic text. The image is not a JSON field or a path. A valid screenshot may still have blocking diagnostics; fix them and render again.
  properties:
    code:
      const: RENDER_FAILED
      description: Present when the screenshot exists but diagnostics contain blocking issues; this does not imply the screenshot is missing.
      type: string
    diagnostics:
      additionalProperties: false
      description: Observed rendering problems; these checks do not prove complete visual correctness.
      properties:
        console_errors:
          description: Up to 50 console error messages or uncaught script errors, at most 1000 characters each; empty if none.
          items:
            description: Captured browser error message.
            type: string
          type: array
        failed_resources:
          description: Up to 50 deduplicated failed requests, HTTP 400+ responses or blocked external resources; empty if none.
          items:
            description: Failure cause or HTTP status with the resource address.
            type: string
          type: array
        out_of_bounds:
          description: Up to 50 descendants extending beyond .slide-stage (document root if absent), allowing 1px tolerance; empty if none. Does not detect all clipping, ellipsis or overlap.
          items:
            additionalProperties: false
            description: One element whose bounds extend outside the stage.
            properties:
              class:
                description: Element class text, when present, truncated to at most 160 characters.
                type: string
              id:
                description: Element ID, omitted when absent.
                type: string
              rect:
                additionalProperties: false
                description: Element bounding rectangle in the page iframe viewport.
                properties:
                  bottom:
                    description: Element's bottom edge relative to the page iframe viewport, in CSS pixels rounded to an integer.
                    type: integer
                  left:
                    description: Element's left edge relative to the page iframe viewport, in CSS pixels rounded to an integer.
                    type: integer
                  right:
                    description: Element's right edge relative to the page iframe viewport, in CSS pixels rounded to an integer.
                    type: integer
                  top:
                    description: Element's top edge relative to the page iframe viewport, in CSS pixels rounded to an integer.
                    type: integer
                required:
                  - left
                  - top
                  - right
                  - bottom
                type: object
              tag:
                description: Lowercase HTML tag name.
                type: string
            required:
              - tag
              - rect
            type: object
          type: array
        overflow:
          additionalProperties: false
          description: Scrollable content size compared to canvas viewport with 1px tolerance; independent of individual element bounds.
          properties:
            horizontal:
              description: Horizontal overflow verdict with content width, canvas width and excess pixels when overflowing.
              type: string
            vertical:
              description: Vertical overflow verdict with content height, canvas height and excess pixels when overflowing.
              type: string
          required:
            - horizontal
            - vertical
          type: object
      required:
        - overflow
        - out_of_bounds
        - console_errors
        - failed_resources
      type: object
    reason:
      description: Explanation of blocking diagnostics requiring page changes and a new render; omitted when no blocking issue was found.
      type: string
    slide_id:
      description: Stable ID of the rendered page.
      type: string
  required:
    - slide_id
    - diagnostics
  type: object
  x-content-kind: json+image
  x-error-schema:
    additionalProperties: true
    description: Failure reply as JSON text instead of the normal result. Optional detail fields appear only when relevant; no ok or data wrapper is returned.
    properties:
      actual:
        description: Observed JSON type, not the field's full value.
        type: string
      call_id:
        description: Original tool call identifier, when bound by Runtime; not a resource ID.
        type: string
      category:
        description: Failure class: transient may be retried; agent_repairable requires correction; user_action_required needs user intervention; conflict requires refreshing state; canceled stops the operation; terminal cannot be repaired by repeating the call.
        enum:
          - transient
          - agent_repairable
          - user_action_required
          - conflict
          - canceled
          - terminal
        type: string
      code:
        description: Machine-readable failure code; use reason and next_action to decide how to recover.
        type: string
      dependency_call_id:
        description: Call ID of the failed prerequisite; this dependent call was not executed.
        type: string
      dependency_resources:
        description: Logical resources written by the failed prerequisite.
        items:
          additionalProperties: false
          description: One prerequisite resource.
          properties:
            part:
              description: Resource kind: manifest, design, outline, spec or html.
              type: string
            slide_id:
              description: Stable page ID when this is a page resource.
              type: string
            type:
              description: Resource scope: deck or slide.
              type: string
          required:
            - type
            - part
          type: object
        type: array
      dependency_tool:
        description: Tool used by the failed prerequisite.
        type: string
      expected:
        description: Expected JSON type, included when a type mismatch is diagnosed.
        type: string
      field:
        description: Path to the invalid input or saved-content field; / denotes the root.
        type: string
      next_action:
        description: Recovery guidance for this failure; follow it before retrying an unchanged operation.
        type: string
      operation:
        description: Operation that failed, usually tool_call.
        type: string
      reason:
        description: Concrete reason this call failed; not a successful business result.
        type: string
      resource:
        additionalProperties: false
        description: Affected resource, when identified.
        properties:
          part:
            description: Affected resource part, such as manifest, outline, design, spec or html.
            type: string
          slide_id:
            description: Affected page's stable ID, when applicable.
            type: string
          type:
            description: Resource scope, such as deck or slide.
            type: string
        required:
          - type
          - part
        type: object
      retryable:
        description: Whether Runtime classifies this failure as automatically retryable; false does not prevent a corrected call after following next_action.
        type: boolean
    required:
      - code
      - category
      - reason
      - retryable
      - next_action
    type: object

TOOL 14 | request_privilege
------------------------------------------------------------------------
description:

        pattern: ^sli_[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$
        type: string
      minItems: 1
      type: array

TOOL 16 | run_command
------------------------------------------------------------------------
description:
Run a restricted project-local command against the current durable project state. Supported reads: ls, cat, head, tail, find, grep, jq, rg, pwd, stat, sed -n, wc, git status, git diff, and git log. The only write form is a confirmed single-file sed -i substitution in execute mode. Read the full exact target text with single-file cat first; read_resource also supplies exact text for outline and HTML, but JSON objects do not authorize text edits. Command output is saved verbatim with domain validation, without formatting. A stale or unseen version is rejected.

input_schema:
  additionalProperties: false
  properties:
    command:
      description: Command text to inspect project files or Git state, using the supported commands and project-local paths. Editing is limited to a single-file sed -i substitution in execute mode after reading the full current target; Runtime requests user approval before execution.
      maxLength: 4096
      minLength: 1
      type: string
  required:
    - command
  type: object

Output contract (JSON Schema for returned text; x-content-kind identifies the successful response content, x-error-schema describes error replies; these annotations are not returned fields):
  additionalProperties: false
  description: Captured command result. Output limits terminate execution; failures may retain partial output. No full-output file or continuation handle is provided.
  properties:

      type: integer

      const: true

      type: boolean
    stderr:
      description: Captured standard error; empty when none was captured.
      type: string
    stdout:
      description: Captured standard output; may be incomplete on failure or truncation.
      type: string

    - stderr
    - exit_code
  type: object
